## Members

<dl>
<dt><a href="#DocxService">DocxService</a></dt>
<dd><p>DOCX feature service: builds documents and parses/metadata-inspects uploads.</p>
</dd>
</dl>

## Functions

<dl>
<dt><a href="#readPartsCapped">readPartsCapped(buffer, limits)</a> ⇒</dt>
<dd><p>Maps the shared OOXML reader&#39;s cap violations to HTTP errors (413 for size, 400 otherwise).</p>
</dd>
<dt><a href="#readBody">readBody(cls)</a> ⇒ <code>Promise&lt;Buffer&lt;ArrayBufferLike&gt;&gt;</code></dt>
<dd><p>Opens the uploaded body, bounded by the scanner cap.
The scanner only opens the source when ClamAV is enabled, so the service must too.</p>
</dd>
<dt><a href="#assertDocxSignature">assertDocxSignature(buffer)</a> ⇒ <code>Promise&lt;void&gt;</code></dt>
<dd><p>Signature must be the plain docx mime; closes the renamed-image and .docm/.dotx holes.</p>
</dd>
<dt><a href="#assertUniqueIds">assertUniqueIds(body)</a> ⇒ <code>{ commentIds: Set&lt;number&gt;; endnoteIds: Set&lt;number&gt;; noteIds: Set&lt;number&gt;; }</code></dt>
<dd><p>Collects and rejects duplicate note/comment ids.</p>
</dd>
<dt><a href="#assertBuildPayload">assertBuildPayload(body)</a></dt>
<dd><p>Validates build-payload cross-field rules the schema cannot express:
svg images must carry a fallback raster, hyperlinks need an allowed url or an anchor,
note/comment references must exist, ids must be unique, row spans must fit,
and the element count is capped to bound memory.</p>
</dd>
<dt><a href="#tables">tables(body)</a> ⇒ <code>Record&lt;string, unknown&gt;[]</code></dt>
<dd><p>Collects every table element anywhere in the payload.</p>
</dd>
<dt><a href="#validateRowSpan">validateRowSpan(table)</a></dt>
<dd><p>Rejects rowSpan values exceeding the rows remaining in the table.</p>
</dd>
<dt><a href="#reportNoteRefs">reportNoteRefs(body, noteIds, endnoteIds, commentIds)</a></dt>
<dd><p>Rejects note/comment references that are not declared.</p>
</dd>
<dt><a href="#checkNoteRef">checkNoteRef(record, noteIds, endnoteIds, commentIds)</a></dt>
<dd><p>Rejects one record&#39;s note/comment references when they are not declared.</p>
</dd>
</dl>

<a name="DocxService"></a>

## DocxService
DOCX feature service: builds documents and parses/metadata-inspects uploads.

**Kind**: global variable  
src: packages/backend/src/resources/docx/docx.service.ts:345  
dist: packages/backend/dist/resources/docx/docx.service.js:261

* [DocxService](#DocxService)
    * [.build(body)](#DocxService+build) ⇒ <code>Promise&lt;Buffer&lt;ArrayBufferLike&gt;&gt;</code>
    * [.extract()](#DocxService+extract) ⇒ <code>Promise&lt;{ format: string; metadata: any; structure: { headings: { level: number; text: string; }[]; hyperlinks: any; images: { alts: string[]; count: number; }; lists: number; tables: any; }; text: string; }&gt;</code>
    * [.metadata()](#DocxService+metadata) ⇒ <code>Promise&lt;any&gt;</code>

<a name="DocxService+build"></a>

### docxService.build(body) ⇒ <code>Promise&lt;Buffer&lt;ArrayBufferLike&gt;&gt;</code>
Builds a .docx from the payload in a worker thread and returns the bytes.
Buffers before returning so a worker failure answers 500, never a truncated 201.
The cache key is the payload hash, so repeated builds are served from the document cache.

**Kind**: instance method of [<code>DocxService</code>](#DocxService)  
**Returns**: <code>Promise&lt;Buffer&lt;ArrayBufferLike&gt;&gt;</code> - The .docx bytes.

<a name="DocxService+extract"></a>

### docxService.extract() ⇒ <code>Promise&lt;{ format: string; metadata: any; structure: { headings: { level: number; text: string; }[]; hyperlinks: any; images: { alts: string[]; count: number; }; lists: number; tables: any; }; text: string; }&gt;</code>

<a name="readPartsCapped"></a>

## readPartsCapped(buffer, limits) ⇒
Maps the shared OOXML reader's cap violations to HTTP errors (413 for size, 400 otherwise).

**Kind**: global function  
**Returns**: The extracted package parts.  

| Param | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| buffer | <code>Buffer&lt;ArrayBufferLike&gt;</code> | The buffered upload. |
| limits |  | Optional per-call cap overrides. |

src: packages/backend/src/resources/docx/docx.service.ts:29

<a name="readBody"></a>

## readBody(cls) ⇒ <code>Promise&lt;Buffer&lt;ArrayBufferLike&gt;&gt;</code>
Opens the uploaded body, bounded by the scanner cap.
The scanner only opens the source when ClamAV is enabled, so the service must too.

**Kind**: global function  
**Returns**: <code>Promise&lt;Buffer&lt;ArrayBufferLike&gt;&gt;</code> - The body bytes, capped.  

| Param | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| cls | <code>NormalizedClsService</code> | Normalized request context. |

src: packages/backend/src/resources/docx/docx.service.ts:50  
dist: packages/backend/dist/resources/docx/docx.service.js:37
<a name="assertDocxSignature"></a>

## assertDocxSignature(buffer) ⇒ <code>Promise&lt;void&gt;</code>
Signature must be the plain docx mime; closes the renamed-image and .docm/.dotx holes.

**Kind**: global function  
**Returns**: <code>Promise&lt;void&gt;</code> - Nothing when the signature is confirmed.  
**Throws**:

- <code>UnsupportedMediaTypeException</code> For non-docx signatures and mismatches.


| Param | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| buffer | <code>Buffer&lt;ArrayBufferLike&gt;</code> | The buffered upload. |

src: packages/backend/src/resources/docx/docx.service.ts:156  
dist: packages/backend/dist/resources/docx/docx.service.js:118
<a name="assertBuildPayload"></a>

## assertBuildPayload(body)
Validates build-payload cross-field rules the schema cannot express:
svg images must carry a fallback raster, hyperlinks need an allowed url or an anchor,
note/comment references must exist, ids must be unique, row spans must fit,
and the element count is capped to bound memory.

**Kind**: global function

| Param | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| table | <code>Record&lt;string, unknown&gt;</code> | The table record. |

<a name="checkNoteRef"></a>

## checkNoteRef(record, noteIds, endnoteIds, commentIds)
Rejects one record's note/comment references when they are not declared.

**Kind**: global function  

| Param | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| record | <code>Record&lt;string, unknown&gt;</code> | The inspected dictionary. |
| noteIds | <code>Set&lt;number&gt;</code> | The declared footnote ids. |
| endnoteIds | <code>Set&lt;number&gt;</code> | The declared endnote ids. |
| commentIds | <code>Set&lt;number&gt;</code> | The declared comment ids. |
